## Pricing Methods
| Pricing Method | Example | How to Set Up | At the till | Limitation |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| #1 Regular Retail | Normal Pricing | Regular Retail  $0.99 | First Item  $0.99 | Mix & Match has to be between items with identical retails. |
| NaN | For example, an item sells for $0.99 | NaN | Second Item  $0.99 | NaN |
| NaN | NaN | NaN | Third  Item  $0.99 | NaN |
| #2 Deal Quantity Split Allowed | If you buy more than 1 item you get a deal price (but you still get the deal price if you only buy one item) | Regular Retail $0.99 | First Item  $0.50 | Mix & Match has to be between items with identical retails. |
| NaN | For example 2 for $0.99 (but if you buy  1 it is $0.50) | Break Qty 2 | Second Item  $0.49 | NaN |
| NaN | NaN | Special Price 0.99 | Third  Item  $0.50 | NaN |
| #3 Deal Quantity Purchase Required | If you buy more than 1 item you get a deal price, but if you buy less than deal quantity you pay regular price (first tier price) | Regular Retail $0.69 | First Item  $0.69 | 1.  Mix & Match has to be between items with identical retails. |
| NaN | For example 2 for $0.99 (but if you buy  1 it is $0.69) | Break Qty 2 | Second Item  $0.30 | NaN |
| NaN | NaN | Special Price 0.99 | Third  Item  $0.69 | 2. POS System can not  enforce that different or specific items are purchased for example by a Coke  and Lays for $1.50 or pay $1.00 for if single unit is bought, customer can buy two Coke at $1.50 or two Lays at $1.50 |
| NaN | Another example “buy 1, get 1 Free” | NaN | NaN | NaN |
| NaN | NaN | Regular Retail $0.69 | First Item  $0.69 | NaN |
| NaN | NaN | Break Qty 2 | Second Item  $0.0 | NaN |
| NaN | NaN | Special Price   $0.69 | NaN | NaN |
| #4 Minimum Purchased Required | After you have purchased a certain number of items, you are able to purchase all of the items for a reduced price. | Regular Retail $0.69 | First Item  $0.69 | Same as Pricing Method # 3 limitations |
| NaN | For example, buy 6 for $3.000 ($0.50 each), but less than 6 are $0.69 each. | Break Qty 6 | Second Item  $0.69 | NaN |
| NaN |  | Special Price 0.50 | Third  Item  $0.69 | NaN |
| NaN |  | NaN | Forth  Item  $0.69 | NaN |
| NaN | NaN | NaN | Fifth  Item  $0.69 | NaN |
| NaN | NaN | NaN | Sixth Item  -$0.45 | NaN |
| #5 Maximum Quantity Specified | You can by a certain number of items at a deal price but any quantity over that will be at regular price (a higher First tier price). | Regular Retail $0.69 | First Item  $0.50 | Same as Pricing Method # 3 limitations |
| NaN | For example, a unit sells for $0.50 each (maximum  3 units), | Break Qty 3 | Second Item  $0.50 | NaN |
| NaN | Each one after 3 is $0.69 each. | Special Price 0.50 | Third  Item  $0.50 | NaN |
| NaN | NaN | NaN | Forth  Item  $0.69 | NaN |
| NaN | NaN | NaN | Fifth  Item  $0.69 | NaN |
| #6 Percentage Off | Percentage Off current retail price | NaN | NaN | NaN |
| #7 Dollar Off | Dollar Off current retail price | NaN | NaN | NaN |
| #8 Random Weight Items | Normal Pricing | NaN | NaN | NaN |
| NaN | For example, an item sells 1 KG for $2.00, | NaN | NaN | NaN |
|  | If  the customer buys | NaN | NaN | NaN |
